# Database Audit - 2026-08-09

Generated from local `.env` connection names. Secrets and row values are intentionally not included.

## Django Model Relations

- Unique current model `db_table` relations found: 60
- Most learner/enrolment/curriculum/engagement models are `managed = False`, so Django migrations do not own those tables.

## Migration State Notes

- `public.django_migrations` contains app migrations that are not present in this checkout, especially for `coach_api`, `curriculum_api`, `quiz_api`, `learner_api`, `engagement_api`, and `enrolment_api`.
- Several migration numbers have branch-style duplicates in the database history, for example multiple `coach_api.0005*` and multiple `curriculum_api.0012*` entries.
- Treat the current database as the source of truth until the repo migration graph is rebuilt or baselined.
